HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001193/B001","root_001193/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0cc4b9e9f4545f50bc32","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:1:7\",\"root_001193\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Fil adı, başarısız planın açığa çıkardığı zayıf muhakemeyi de çağrıştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000844/B001","root_000844/B004","root_001193/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2828eed4a55b32030afe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:1:6\",\"root_000844\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"105:1:7\",\"root_001193\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Fil ile anılan topluluk, hayvan gücü, insanlar ve planın birbirine bağlandığı tek bir işleyen düzenek gibi görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_000531/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2db371e89b819ef6d4a4","text":"1. **Seeing becomes causal recognition.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `تَرَ` carries `ر ء ي` B001, “seeing by eye or insight,” and B002, “opinion and reflective judgment.” **Structural cue:** the interrogative `كَيْفَ` is followed by an ordered answer: the plot is displaced, aerial groups are sent, they cast stones, and the actors reach a new material state. **Assigned role:** B001 makes the outcome perceptible, while B002 makes the sequence an object of reasoning. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies effects and their order; I infer that those effects make the otherwise unstated operation cognitively visible. **Reading change:** `أَلَمْ تَرَ` need not be restricted to eyewitness sight; it asks the addressee to reconstruct agency from a causal demonstration.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001167/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3483f7d7ec18d7da6201","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:1:4\",\"root_001167\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Başta genel bırakılan yapma eylemi, planın etkisizleştirilmesinden grubun maddi dönüşümüne uzanan aşamalı bir işlem olarak açılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_000531/B002","root_000531/B012","root_000531/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4585b59a1c8990843dbd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:1:2\",\"root_000531\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B012\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Görmek, gözle tanıklıkla sınırlı kalmaz; sonraki aşamalardan hareketle eyleyen gücü ve nedensel düzeni kavramaya çağrıya dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001193/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4ae5a69660bc32581173","text":"4. **The elephant label contains an internal reversal of judgment.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ٱلْفِيلِ` directly realizes `ف ي ل` B004, “the elephant,” while dormant B001 is “slackness and weakness,” specifically weak or mistaken judgment. **Structural cue:** the group named by the elephant is immediately followed by `كَيْدَهُمْ` being placed in `تَضْلِيلٍ`. **Assigned role:** B004 supplies the group’s conspicuous material affiliation; B001 supplies a latent cognitive defect exposed by the failure of its strategy. **Abductive move:** I allow a dormant branch of the same root to color the animal label because the next ayah explicitly evaluates the group’s planning; this paronomastic link is exploratory, not a replacement of “elephant.” **Reading change:** the apparent emblem of massive force simultaneously forecasts failed discernment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_66dea4dd36a367645fee","text":"The final جعل in 105:5 was the strongest retrospective correction: it made 105:1's \"فَعَلَ رَبُّكَ\" less like a single deed and more like a chain of transformations. 105:3's أبابيل also revised \"أصحاب الفيل\": the elephant-company was not only defeated by birds, but by a rival pattern of association, scattered and successive rather than heavy and centralized. The missing ك ي ف inventory remained important: the \"how\" had to be supplied structurally from the sequence, not lexically by invented branches.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000532/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_70f6c09a0d2a589d6787","text":"3. **`رَبُّكَ` is activated as completing care as well as authority.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `رَبُّكَ` carries `ر ب ب` B001, “lordship, ownership, and authority,” and B002, “repair, nurture, and completion”; the opening repeats `ر ح م` B001. **Structural cue:** the second-person bond in `رَبُّكَ` stands between the invitation to see and the completed chain of intervention. **Assigned role:** B001 identifies the authoritative actor; B002 explains the actor’s sustained management; contextual mercy supplies the disposition under which that management is read. **Abductive move:** the packet does not name what is being preserved, so I infer only that defeating the presented plot is maintenance within the Lord-addressee relation. **Reading change:** `رَبُّكَ` becomes the title of an involved governor who carries a counter-process to completion, not merely a rank of power.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002","root_000532/B001","root_000532/B002","root_000913/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_83f42d8ef4d0fbace7e5","text":"2. **Lordship as corrective administration.** Lexical evidence: ر ب ب B001, lordship, ownership, and authority; ر ب ب B002, repair, nurture, and completion; ج ع ل B002 in 105:2 and 105:5, turning something into a state; ض ل ل B001/B003, going astray from aim and losing/failing to locate. Structural cue: \"رَبُّكَ\" occurs before two instances of جعل that convert their كيد into تضليل and then convert them into عصف مأكول. Assigned role: ر ب ب B001 supplies sovereign capacity; ر ب ب B002 supplies a caretaker's power to set a damaged order right. Abductive move: I supply that the two later make/turn operations are administrative acts of the Lord, not separate narrative facts. Reading change: the Lord's \"doing\" reads as a corrective governance of states and outcomes, not only an overwhelming blow.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_000531/B012","root_000531/B013","root_001167/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_89c3e7d7b2fa0a7221bf","text":"1. **The ayah as an instruction to inspect a mechanism, not merely recall an event.** Lexical evidence: ر ء ي B001, seeing by eye or insight; ر ء ي B012, showing and making seen; ر ء ي B013, \"tell me\" formula; ف ع ل B001, bringing an act into being. ك ي ف has no accepted non-contaminated branch inventory in the packet, so I use it only as the surface interrogative \"how\" and do not assign a branch ID. Structural cue: 105:1 asks about manner before 105:2-5 give sequential operations: making the plot lost, sending birds, throwing stones, making them chaff. Assigned role: ر ء ي makes the reader/viewer an observer of staged causation; ف ع ل names the whole sequence as an effected act. Abductive move: I infer that the later ayat are the answer to \"how\"; the packet supplies the order, while I supply the forensic reading of that order. Reading change: \"أَلَمْ تَرَ\" reads less as simple \"did you not see\" and more as \"watch the way the action is made visible.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B001","root_001193/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_95a66082c01d70e7bbc2","text":"4. **Mighty animal force inverted by airborne and granular force.** Lexical evidence: ف ي ل B004, elephant; ط ي ر B001 in 105:3, airborne lightness and birds; ء ب ل B003, successive or scattered groups; ر م ي B001 and ح ج ر B003 in 105:4, casting hard stones. Structural cue: a single emblematic ground animal in 105:1 is answered by many airborne groups and small hard projectiles in 105:3-4. Assigned role: the elephant supplies mass and ground-bound prestige; the birds and stones supply distributed vertical force. Abductive move: I infer a reversal between the mode of confidence and the mode of defeat; the packet supplies animal/bird/projectile imagery, while I supply the directional inversion. Reading change: the ayah starts to read as the setup of a material reversal: weight below is overcome by lightness above carrying hardness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000844/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9a87f37392f1cb10fbd9","text":"5. **Companionship becomes operational coupling.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `أَصْحَٰبِ` carries `ص ح ب` B001, “companionship and close association,” including possession or charge. **Structural cue:** the plural possessive in `كَيْدَهُمْ` treats the elephant-associated people as one planning body, while `طَيْرًا أَبَابِيلَ` later presents a counter-collective organized in groups. **Assigned role:** B001 binds people, animal power, and plan into one operating formation. **Abductive move:** the packet gives association and a collective strategy; I infer that the association is functional rather than a loose label. **Reading change:** `أَصْحَابِ ٱلْفِيلِ` reads as a coupled human-animal apparatus confronted by a differently organized animal collective.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000532/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b7239a7fd1e6f0a0663b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:1:5\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rablik, yalnız üstün yetki değil, karşı süreci sonuna kadar yürüten düzeltici ve tamamlayıcı yönetim olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001193/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c462dce9dfe83fd0dccd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:1:7\",\"root_001193\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Filin ağır ve gösterişli gücü, yukarıdan gelen hafif taşıyıcılar ve küçük sert cisimlerle tersine çevrilecek maddi karşıtlığı baştan kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000844/B001","root_000844/B004","root_001193/B001","root_001193/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dbb307caa7a00a16e5f2","text":"3. **\"Companions of the elephant\" as exposed dependence.** Lexical evidence: ص ح ب B001, companionship and close association; ص ح ب B004, making something accompany or carrying it along; ف ي ل B004, the elephant; ف ي ل B001, slackness and weak judgment. Structural cue: the human group is identified by companionship with the animal before their كيد is placed in تضليل. Assigned role: ص ح ب binds the group identity to what it accompanies; ف ي ل B004 supplies visible bulk, while ف ي ل B001 supplies a latent weakness of judgment activated by the later lost plot. Abductive move: I infer that the phrase does not merely label ownership but reveals the support system by which they understand themselves. Reading change: \"أصحاب الفيل\" becomes a diagnosis of dependence on heavy animal power and weak discernment, not a neutral historical tag.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002","root_001167/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f92463dc72f06f44b88a","text":"2. **The broad verb `فَعَلَ` unfolds into a staged operation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `فَعَلَ` carries `ف ع ل` B001, “bringing an act into being”; contextual `ج ع ل` B002 is “turning something into a state,” and `ر س ل` B001 is dispatch/release. **Structural cue:** the initially unspecified act is subsequently distributed over two `جعل` clauses with `أرسل` and `ترميهم` between them. **Assigned role:** B001 names the whole act, the two state-transformations mark its abstract and material endpoints, and dispatch supplies the intervening means. **Abductive move:** the packet gives the sequence; I infer that the later clauses unpack the earlier `فَعَلَ` rather than merely list unrelated events. **Reading change:** the Lord’s act reads as an organized process that changes strategy first and actors last, not as one undifferentiated blow.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002","root_001193/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f98f2c13ff567d90cb01","text":"105:2 supplied the sharpest surprise: `كَيْدَهُمْ ... تَضْلِيلٍ` activated the weak-judgment branch `ف ي ل` B001 inside the otherwise literal elephant label. Then 105:5’s repeated `ج ع ل` B002 retrospectively organized `فَعَلَ` into paired state changes, while 105:3’s grouped birds showed that `أَصْحَاب` names one operational collective facing another rather than a merely incidental association.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The open question about what the Lord did becomes a precise reversal in which the companions' own designed plan is made to lose direction.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_1d08e8ef4c0adb07de2c","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The focus's attached elephant-centered bulk is reread against dispatched, light, distributed groups and then against the dissolution of companionship into scattered residue.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/1","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_a5dcf02035d5032a1b45","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
